# Complete context catalogue — 20 October 2026

Owner-requested dated preservation. Live source and owning plan TODOs remain authoritative.
This index is navigation, not a second work queue or a new mandatory evidence workflow.

## Read order

1. Read `../FULL_COMPREHENSIVE_AGENT_HANDOFF_2026-10-20.md` consecutively in bounded ranges. Its reading map identifies every embedded body.
2. Read live AGENTS, plan README/queue, current per-plan TODOs and the reviewed correctness-first plan. Dated TODO bodies preserve earlier work, not new duplicate tasks.
3. For original V1–V5.1 rationale, read the historical appendix and recovered design bodies below. The 94-source nested archive is complete within the recovered historical body; missing original Git ancestry/later aggregates are not manufactured.
4. Follow the canonical-source rows for all exact specs, research, prior art, decision registers, model dossiers, acceptance suites and public documents. Their original paths remain live owners. Sources outside docs/plans are fully copied here, not merely linked.
5. Follow run-note rows for every retained documented experiment, failure, review and interrupted run. Draft-patch/continuation rows preserve historical and unaccepted proposals; do not apply or execute them automatically.

All frozen bodies are UTF-8 text enclosed in fences. Resolve embedded relative links against their **original** path. File/line ranges below refer to the containing archive volume. Read complete bodies progressively; do not use headings or a truncated tool result as a substitute.

## Coverage and limits

- Canonical source capture includes every file then present under `.agents/docs`, `.agents/plans`, `.agents/research`, `.agents/prior-art` and public `docs`, plus AGENTS/root README. It excludes this newly generated handoff directory to prevent recursive archives.
- All 624 then-existing MAIN `.agents/runs/**/*.md` bodies and all 220 retained `.patch` files are preserved. Their status remains whatever the actual owning source/result says.
- Fifteen recoverable historical design bodies are preserved from their recorded Git refs, including the actual40447-byte pre-product README from `be95424`, not only its later pointer.
- Selected unique paused source/harness/metadata, the fresh teacher-ID JSON/text, and MAIN plus both ROOT isolated source diffs/untracked files are copied. The paused collector and its source/license notices are included.
- This is complete documentation preservation for the enumerated available sources, not a copy of model weights, SDKs, containers, every executable/capture/DSO or every older replay harness. Existing caches/run directories/worktrees must remain. Full raw numerical payloads and runtime environments are still external dependencies identified by the continuation chapters.
- Original33-node ancestry, later129/191-source aggregates and the full70-report set remain unrecovered; some early reports survive inside the older94-source body. Do not infer missing source from an old title/hash.
- Exact body bytes were compared with their captured source. A checksum proves preservation, not execution or numerical correctness. `ARCHIVE_CONTENTS.json` records byte counts, exact body locations and digests for this one-time check.
